## Architecture Source

- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

  26:           PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
  27:           PR_HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
  28:         shell: bash
  29:         run: |
  30:           set -euo pipefail
  31:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  32:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  33:           test "$GITHUB_EVENT_NAME" = "pull_request"
  34:           test "$GITHUB_HEAD_REF" = "ingest/staging"
  35:           test "$GITHUB_BASE_REF" = "main"
  36:           gh api "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/pulls/$PR_NUMBER" \
  37:             --jq '.user.login + "\t" + .head.sha + "\t" + .head.ref + "\t" + .base.ref' \
  38:             > pr_identity.tsv
  39:           expected="$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  40:           actual="$(cut -f1 pr_identity.tsv)"
  41:           head="$(cut -f2 pr_identity.tsv)"
  42:           source="$(cut -f3 pr_identity.tsv)"
  43:           target="$(cut -f4 pr_identity.tsv)"
  44:           test "$actual" = "$expected"
  45:           test "$head" = "$PR_HEAD_SHA"
  46:           test "$source" = "ingest/staging"
  47:           test "$target" = "main"
  48:           gh pr checks "$PR_NUMBER" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --required --watch
  49:           gh pr merge "$PR_NUMBER" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --auto --squash

### \.github\workflows\validate-ingest.yml
   1: name: validate-ingest
   2: on:
   3:   push:
   4:     branches: [ingest/staging]
   5: permissions: {}
   6: jobs:
   7:   validate:
   8:     permissions:
   9:       contents: read
  10:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  11:     steps:
  12:       # actions/checkout v4.2.2
  13:       - uses: actions/checkout@11bd71901bbe5b1630ceea73d27597364c9af683
  14:         with:
  15:           fetch-depth: 0
  16:       # actions/setup-python v5.4.0
  17:       - uses: actions/setup-python@e797f83bcb11b83ae66e0230d6156d7c80228e7c
  18:         with:
  19:           python-version: '3.12'
  20:           cache: pip
  21:           cache-dependency-path: requirements.txt
  22:       - run: python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -r requirements.txt
  23:       - env:
  24:           EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN: ${{ vars.RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN }}
  25:           EXPECTED_REPOSITORY: TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results
  26:           GITHUB_BASE_SHA: ${{ github.event.before }}
  27:           GITHUB_HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
  28:           ZERO_SHA: '0000000000000000000000000000000000000000'
  29:         shell: bash
  30:         run: |
  31:           set -euo pipefail
  32:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  33:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  34:           test "$GITHUB_REF" = "refs/heads/ingest/staging"
  35:           test "$GITHUB_ACTOR" = "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  36:           base="$GITHUB_BASE_SHA"
  37:           if [ "$base" = "$ZERO_SHA" ]; then
  38:             git rev-parse --verify --quiet origin/main^{commit} >/dev/null
  39:             base="$(git merge-base "$GITHUB_HEAD_SHA" origin/main)"
  40:           fi
  41:           git rev-parse --verify --quiet "$base^{commit}" >/dev/null
  42:           git rev-parse --verify --quiet "$GITHUB_HEAD_SHA^{commit}" >/dev/null
  43:           python tools/validate_result.py --base "$base" --head "$GITHUB_HEAD_SHA"
  44:   rebuild-indexes:
  45:     needs: validate
  46:     permissions:
  47:       contents: write
  48:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  49:     steps:
  50:       # actions/checkout v4.2.2
  51:       - uses: actions/checkout@11bd71901bbe5b1630ceea73d27597364c9af683
  52:         with:
  53:           fetch-depth: 0
  54:       # actions/setup-python v5.4.0
  55:       - uses: actions/setup-python@e797f83bcb11b83ae66e0230d6156d7c80228e7c
  56:         with:
  57:           python-version: '3.12'
  58:           cache: pip
  59:           cache-dependency-path: requirements.txt
  60:       - run: python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -r requirements.txt
  61:       - env:
  62:           EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN: ${{ vars.RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN }}
  63:           EXPECTED_REPOSITORY: TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results
  64:           STAGING_BRANCH: ingest/staging
  65:         shell: bash
  66:         run: |
  67:           set -euo pipefail
  68:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  69:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  70:           test "$GITHUB_ACTOR" = "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  71:           git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
  72:           git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
  73:           for attempt in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  74:             git fetch --no-tags origin "$STAGING_BRANCH"
  75:             latest="$(git rev-parse "origin/$STAGING_BRANCH")"
  76:             git checkout --detach "$latest"
  77:             python tools/validate_result.py --base "$latest" --head "$latest"
  78:             rm -f data/index.tsv data/by_author.tsv data/best_solutions.tsv data/runs.json
  79:             python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
  80:             git add -- data/index.tsv data/by_author.tsv data/best_solutions.tsv data/runs.json
  81:             if git diff --cached --quiet; then
  82:               exit 0
  83:             fi
  84:             if ! git diff --cached --quiet -- . ':!data/index.tsv' ':!data/by_author.tsv' ':!data/best_solutions.tsv' ':!data/runs.json'; then
  85:               exit 1
  86:             fi
  87:             git commit -m "chore: rebuild CayleyPy result indexes"
  88:             if git push origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH"; then
  89:               exit 0
  90:             fi
  91:           done
  92:           exit 1

### \tools\build_indexes.py
   1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2: """Deterministically derive public indexes from immutable CayleyPy v1 records."""
   3: from __future__ import annotations
   4: 
   5: import argparse
   6: from dataclasses import dataclass
   7: from datetime import datetime
   8: import json
   9: import re
  10: from pathlib import Path
  11: from typing import Any, Iterable
  12: 
  13: try:
  14:     from tools.validate_result import (
  15:         ValidationError as RecordValidationError,
  16:         load_schema,
  17:         validate_integrity,
  18:         validate_schema,
  19:     )
  20: except ModuleNotFoundError:
  21:     from validate_result import ValidationError as RecordValidationError, load_schema, validate_integrity, validate_schema
  22: 
  23: UUID7 = re.compile(
  24:     r"^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$"
  25: )
  26: RESULT_PATH = re.compile(
  27:     r"^results/v1/([a-z0-9._-]{1,128})/([a-z0-9._-]{1,128})/"
  28:     r"(0|[1-9][0-9]*)/(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/([0-9a-f-]{36})\.json$"
  29: )
  30: OUTPUT_NAMES = (
  31:     "index.tsv",
  32:     "by_author.tsv",
  33:     "best_solutions.tsv",
  34:     "runs.json",
  35: )
  36: INDEX_HEADER = (
  37:     "competition",
  38:     "puzzle_type",
  39:     "puzzle_id",
  40:     "solution_length",
  41:     "solved_depth",
  42:     "submission_id",
  43:     "idempotency_key",
  44:     "submitted_at",
  45:     "author_name",
  46:     "kaggle_username",
  47:     "run_id",
  48:     "final_orientation",
  49:     "model_class",
  50:     "output_dim",
  51:     "model_sha256",
  52:     "solver_commit",
  53:     "record_path",
  54: )
  55: 
  56: 
  57: class IndexBuildError(ValueError):
  58:     """Stable fail-closed index build error."""
  59: 
  60: 
  61: def canonical(value: Any) -> str:
  62:     return json.dumps(
  63:         value,
  64:         ensure_ascii=False,
  65:         sort_keys=True,
  66:         separators=(",", ":"),
  67:         allow_nan=False,
  68:     )
  69: 
  70: 
  71: def safe_segment(value: str) -> str:
  72:     output = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9._-]+", "-", value.lower()).strip("-")
  73:     if not output or len(output) > 128:
  74:         raise IndexBuildError("PATH_SEGMENT")
  75:     return output
  76: 
  77: 
  78: def _required_dict(value: Any, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  79:     if not isinstance(value, dict):
  80:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
  81:     return value
  82: 
  83: 
  84: def _required_string(value: Any, code: str) -> str:
  85:     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
  86:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
  87:     return value
  88: 
  89: 
  90: def _required_integer(value: Any, code: str) -> int:
  91:     if type(value) is not int:
  92:         raise IndexBuildError(code)
  93:     return value
  94: 
  95: 
  96: def _formula_after_ignored_prefix(value: str) -> bool:
  97:     index = 0
  98:     while index < len(value):
  99:         character = value[index]
 100:         if not (character.isspace() or ord(character) < 32 or ord(character) == 127):
 101:             break
 102:         index += 1
 103:     return value[index:index + 1] in "=+-@"
 104: 
 105: 
 106: def tsv_cell(value: Any) -> str:
 107:     """Encode one TSV cell without row or spreadsheet-formula injection."""
 108:     text = "" if value is None else str(value)
 109:     dangerous = _formula_after_ignored_prefix(text)
 110:     escaped = []
 111:     for character in text:
 112:         codepoint = ord(character)
 113:         if character == "\\":
 114:             escaped.append("\\\\")
 115:         elif codepoint < 32 or codepoint == 127:
 116:             escaped.append(f"\\u{codepoint:04x}")
 117:         else:
 118:             escaped.append(character)
 119:     encoded = "".join(escaped)
 120:     return "'" + encoded if dangerous else encoded
 121: 
 122: 
 123: def tsv_payload(header: tuple[str, ...], rows: Iterable[tuple[Any, ...]]) -> bytes:
 124:     lines = ["\t".join(header)]
 125:     lines.extend("\t".join(tsv_cell(value) for value in row) for row in rows)
 126:     return ("\n".join(lines) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
 127: 
 128: 
 129: @dataclass(frozen=True)
 130: class ResultRow:

 132:     submission_id: str
 133:     envelope: dict[str, Any]
 134: 
 135:     @property
 136:     def competition(self) -> str:
 137:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("competition"), "PROVENANCE")
 138: 
 139:     @property

 141:         return _required_string(self.envelope.get("puzzle_type"), "PROVENANCE")
 142:

 146:

 150:

 153:         return _required_integer(self.solution.get("length"), "PROVENANCE")
 154:

 166:

 170:

 173:         return _required_string(self.author.get("name"), "PROVENANCE")

 189:

 192:         return _required_string(
 193:             self.orientation.get("final_orientation"), "PROVENANCE"
 194:         )
 195: 
 196:     @property

 198:         return _required_dict(self.envelope.get("profile"), "PROVENANCE")
 199:

 203: 
 204:     @property

 207:

 210:         manifest = _required_dict(self.model.get("manifest"), "PROVENANCE")

 212:

 216:

 220:

### README
   1: # CayleyPy Beam Results
   2: 
   3: `ingest/staging` is an append-only staging branch for canonical CayleyPy v1 result records. The only accepted change type is an added regular file at:
   4: 
   5: `results/v1/<safe-competition>/<safe-puzzle-type>/<puzzle-id>/<UTC-date>/<uuidv7>.json`
   6: 
   7: The validator independently applies the exact v1 JSON Schema, canonical SHA-256/idempotency checks, bounded permutation replay, reflection provenance and inverse checks, model-head parity, and a complete `HEAD` duplicate scan. It rejects edits, deletions, symlinks, executable result files, non-server-derived paths, malformed wrappers, non-canonical record bytes, and records over the envelope limit.
   8: 
   9: Local gate:
  10: 
  11: ```bash
  12: python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
  13: python tools/validate_result.py --base <trusted-base-sha> --head <candidate-head-sha>
  14: ```
  15: 
  16: The repository stores only claimed author metadata; it does not authenticate authors. The staging workflow validates every pushed App commit independently and deterministically rebuilds only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`, `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json` from the latest full staging state. It retries index publication after concurrent commits. Auto-merge is disabled by default: it can run only after an operator sets `ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE=true`, sets the exact `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` repository variable, and applies the `cayleypy-ingest-approved` label to the same-repository staging PR.
  17: 
  18: Derived indexes are generated from the immutable records:
  19: 
  20: ```bash
  21: python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
  22: ```
  23: 
  24: The builder changes only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`,
  25: `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json`; existing legacy `data/`
  26: subdirectories and every `results/v1` record remain untouched. Index generation
  27: fails closed on unexpected result paths, malformed wrappers, duplicate
  28: submission/idempotency keys, and conflicting immutable run provenance. TSV

````

### .github/workflows/merge-ingest.yml
   1: name: merge-ingest
   2: on:
   3:   pull_request:
   4:     branches: [main]
   5:     types: [opened, synchronize, labeled, reopened]
   6: permissions: {}
   7: jobs:
   8:   enable-auto-merge:
   9:     if: >-
  10:       github.repository == 'TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results' &&
  11:       github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository &&
  12:       github.event.pull_request.head.ref == 'ingest/staging' &&
  13:       github.event.pull_request.base.ref == 'main' &&
  14:       github.event.pull_request.user.login == vars.RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN &&
  15:       contains(github.event.pull_request.labels.*.name, 'cayleypy-ingest-approved') &&
  16:       vars.ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE == 'true'
  17:     permissions:
  18:       contents: write
  19:       pull-requests: write
  20:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  21:     steps:
  22:       - env:
  23:           GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
  24:           EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN: ${{ vars.RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN }}
  25:           EXPECTED_REPOSITORY: TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results
  26:           PR_NUMBER: ${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}
  27:           PR_HEAD_SHA: ${{ github.event.pull_request.head.sha }}
  28:         shell: bash
  29:         run: |
  30:           set -euo pipefail
  31:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  32:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  33:           test "$GITHUB_EVENT_NAME" = "pull_request"
  34:           test "$GITHUB_HEAD_REF" = "ingest/staging"
  35:           test "$GITHUB_BASE_REF" = "main"
  36:           gh api "repos/$GITHUB_REPOSITORY/pulls/$PR_NUMBER" \
  37:             --jq '.user.login + "\t" + .head.sha + "\t" + .head.ref + "\t" + .base.ref' \
  38:             > pr_identity.tsv
  39:           expected="$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  40:           actual="$(cut -f1 pr_identity.tsv)"
  41:           head="$(cut -f2 pr_identity.tsv)"
  42:           source="$(cut -f3 pr_identity.tsv)"
  43:           target="$(cut -f4 pr_identity.tsv)"
  44:           test "$actual" = "$expected"
  45:           test "$head" = "$PR_HEAD_SHA"
  46:           test "$source" = "ingest/staging"
  47:           test "$target" = "main"
  48:           gh pr checks "$PR_NUMBER" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --required --watch
  49:           gh pr merge "$PR_NUMBER" --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" --auto --squash
### tools/validate_result.py
   1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2: """Fail-closed validator for append-only CayleyPy v1 staging records."""
   3: from __future__ import annotations
   4: 
   5: import argparse
   6: import hashlib
   7: import json
   8: import re
   9: import subprocess
  10: import sys
  11: from datetime import datetime
  12: from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
  13: from typing import Any, Iterable
  14: 
  15: from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator, FormatChecker
  16: 
  17: MAX_ENVELOPE_BYTES = 256 * 1024
  18: MAX_STATE_LENGTH = 120
  19: MAX_MOVE_COUNT = 256
  20: MAX_PATH_LENGTH = 4096
  21: UUID7 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$")
  22: RESULT_PATH = re.compile(r"^results/v1/([a-z0-9._-]{1,128})/([a-z0-9._-]{1,128})/(0|[1-9][0-9]*)/(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/([0-9a-f-]{36})\.json$")
  23: 
  24: 
  25: class ValidationError(Exception):
  26:     def __init__(self, code: str, detail: str = "") -> None:
  27:         self.code, self.detail = code, detail
  28:         super().__init__(f"{code}{': ' + detail if detail else ''}")
  29: 
  30: 
  31: def canonical(value: Any) -> str:
  32:     return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
  33: 
  34: 
  35: def digest(value: Any) -> str:
  36:     return hashlib.sha256(canonical(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
  37: 
  38: 
  39: def digest_text(value: str) -> str:
  40:     return hashlib.sha256(value.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
  41: 
  42: 
  43: def safe_segment(value: str) -> str:
  44:     out = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9._-]+", "-", value.lower()).strip("-")
  45:     if not out or len(out) > 128:
  46:         raise ValidationError("PATH_SEGMENT")
  47:     return out
  48: 
  49: 
  50: def error(code: str, detail: str = "") -> None:
  51:     raise ValidationError(code, detail)
  52: 
  53: 
  54: def load_schema(root: Path) -> Draft202012Validator:
  55:     schema = json.loads((root / "schemas/result-v1.schema.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  56:     return Draft202012Validator(schema, format_checker=FormatChecker())
  57: 
  58: 
  59: def validate_schema(envelope: Any, validator: Draft202012Validator) -> None:
  60:     issues = sorted(validator.iter_errors({"schema_version": 1, "results": [envelope]}), key=lambda issue: list(issue.absolute_path))
  61:     if issues:
  62:         issue = issues[0]
  63:         location = "/".join(str(part) for part in issue.absolute_path)
  64:         error("SCHEMA", f"{location}:{issue.validator}")
  65: 
  66: 
  67: def valid_state(values: Any, n: int) -> bool:
  68:     return isinstance(values, list) and len(values) == n and all(type(v) is int and 0 <= v < n for v in values)
  69: 
  70: def valid_permutation(values: Any, n: int) -> bool:
  71:     return isinstance(values, list) and len(values) == n and all(type(v) is int and 0 <= v < n for v in values) and len(set(values)) == n
  72: 
  73: 
  74: def replay(initial: list[int], path: list[str], generators: dict[str, list[int]], n: int) -> list[int]:
  75:     state = list(initial)
  76:     for move in path:
  77:         permutation = generators.get(move)
  78:         if permutation is None:
  79:             error("REPLAY_MOVE", move)
  80:         state = [state[source] for source in permutation]
  81:     return state
  82: 
  83: 
  84: def invert_path(path: list[str], generators: dict[str, list[int]], n: int) -> list[str]:
  85:     by_permutation: dict[tuple[int, ...], list[str]] = {}
  86:     for name, permutation in generators.items():
  87:         by_permutation.setdefault(tuple(permutation), []).append(name)
  88:     output: list[str] = []
  89:     for move in reversed(path):
  90:         permutation = generators.get(move)
  91:         if permutation is None:
  92:             error("REFLECTION_MOVE", move)
  93:         inverse = [0] * n
  94:         for target, source in enumerate(permutation):
  95:             inverse[source] = target
  96:         names = by_permutation.get(tuple(inverse), [])
  97:         if len(names) != 1:
  98:             error("REFLECTION_INVERSE", move)
  99:         output.append(names[0])
 100:     return output
 101: 
 102: 
 103: def semantic(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 104:     return {key: value for key, value in envelope.items() if key not in {"client_submission_id", "run_id", "idempotency_key", "submitted_at"}}
 105: 
 106: 
 107: def validate_integrity(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 108:     if len(canonical(envelope).encode("utf-8")) > MAX_ENVELOPE_BYTES:
 109:         error("ENVELOPE_SIZE")
 110:     proof = envelope["proof"]
 111:     manifest = envelope["model"]["manifest"]
 112:     n = manifest["state_len"]
 113:     if n < 1 or n > MAX_STATE_LENGTH or manifest["num_classes"] != n:
 114:         error("STATE_LENGTH")
 115:     generators = proof["generators"]
 116:     if len(generators) > MAX_MOVE_COUNT:
 117:         error("PROOF_BOUNDS")
 118:     if any(not valid_permutation(permutation, n) for permutation in generators.values()):
 119:         error("PERMUTATION")
 120:     if not valid_state(proof["initial_state"], n) or not valid_state(proof["central_state"], n):
 121:         error("STATE_PERMUTATION")

## 17. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T20:27:34.910Z` · source line `57`

```text
The focused tests pass, but the workflow composition has a release-blocking trigger-loop problem: derived-index pushes use `GITHUB_TOKEN`, whose workflow events are suppressed, while the promotion gate expects checks on the updated PR head. I’m checking the remaining race implications and will return exact file references.
```

## 18. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T20:28:17.755Z` · source line `70`

```text
**Verdict: BLOCK — do not ship Task6.** Focused tests pass (`24 passed`), but the Actions protocol cannot reliably promote an indexed staging head and does not guarantee lossless acceptance for 100 concurrent publishers.

1. **P0 — `GITHUB_TOKEN` index pushes suppress the workflow events needed to validate/promote their new SHA.**  
   The rebuild job commits and pushes with `${{ github.token }}` ([validate-ingest.yml:46-47, 71-89]), then treats that push as completion. GitHub suppresses normal workflow triggers caused by `GITHUB_TOKEN`. Therefore the derived-index SHA gets neither a `validate-ingest` run nor the `pull_request:synchronize` promotion run. A PR’s head changes to an unchecked SHA, so auto-merge will normally wait indefinitely; if it did merge based on the former SHA, it could merge before indexes are present. This directly contradicts the README’s claimed validated/derived staging path ([README.md:16]).

2. **P0 — if suppressed events were enabled or a non-`GITHUB_TOKEN` credential were substituted, the workflow rejects its own index commit.**  
   The next staging validation requires every push actor to equal `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` ([validate-ingest.yml:32-35]), but rebuild commits are explicitly authored/pushed as `github-actions[bot]` ([validate-ingest.yml:71-72]). Further, the append-only validator rejects the derived `data/*` modifications ([validate_result.py:241-247]). Thus the two workflows have no valid event model that both validates record pushes and accepts the system’s own derived commits.

3. **P0 — no concrete PR creation or promotion lifecycle exists.**  
   [merge-ingest.yml](D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-beam-results-ingest-results\.github\workflows\merge-ingest.yml:1) only observes an already-existing PR and enables auto-merge ([lines 8-49]). It never creates/updates the required `ingest/staging → main` PR, applies `cayleypy-ingest-approved`, or proves that its head is the fully indexed SHA. The README delegates all of that to an operator ([README.md:16]); this is not an autonomous Task6 promotion path.

4. **P1 — 100 simultaneous writers are not guaranteed accepted or eventually indexed.**  
   There is no ingestion queue, serialization primitive, durable retry, or client-side non-fast-forward retry protocol. GitHub only accepts one direct branch update at a time; 99 concurrent writers can receive non-fast-forward failure and this implementation has no mechanism to retain/replay them. Index rebuilds each retry only five immediate times ([validate-ingest.yml:73-92]), without backoff or a final reconciler; a collision storm can exhaust all workers and leave records present but indexes stale. “No concurrency” preserves runs per pushed commit ([tests/test_workflow_static.py:9,19]), but does not make publications lossless.

5. **P1 — required-check configuration is an unguarded self-deadlock risk.**  
   The promotion job waits on `gh pr checks --required --watch` ([merge-ingest.yml:48]) from within a PR workflow. If `merge-ingest / enable-auto-merge` is itself configured as required, it waits for itself forever. The repository contains no documented branch-rule contract or test preventing this. Separately, Point 1 leaves the generated index head with no required check at all.

6. **P1 — “exact App identity” is login-only, not an App installation identity.**  
   Staging validates `GITHUB_ACTOR == RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` ([validate-ingest.yml:24, 32-35]); promotion checks the PR author login ([merge-ingest.yml:14, 36-47]). There is no verified GitHub App ID/installation identity, no event sender type assertion, and no binding of the PR’s latest indexed commit to the trusted publisher. Login matching is better than no gate, but does not satisfy an “exact App identity” contract.

7. **P2 — full-state integrity is strong for records, but it does not certify the checked-in indexes match that state before promotion.**  
   `validate_range` correctly scans all `results/v1` records and enforces duplicate checks ([validate_result.py:239-255]); the builder independently revalidates records ([build_indexes.py:310-343]). But validation does not regenerate/compare `data/*`; rebuilding happens later in a separate write job. That split is precisely what creates the promotion race above.

Positive observations: workflows use default-deny permissions with narrow job grants; action revisions are pinned; no `pull_request_target`/`workflow_run`; the PR job does not check out or execute PR code; and record/index validation is generally fail-closed. These do not offset the blocking protocol defects.

Minimum ship condition: use one explicitly designed trusted publisher/queue that serializes record commits and index generation, validates the exact final indexed commit, then creates/updates the PR with a non-suppressed trusted App credential (or an explicit workflow-dispatch/repository-dispatch handoff). Define a separate validation mode allowing only deterministic `data/*` derivations, make the PR check run on the final SHA, and document/assert that the auto-merge-enabler job is never required.


```

  43:           STAGING_BRANCH: ingest/staging

  45:         shell: bash
  46:         run: |
  47:           set -euo pipefail
  48:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  49:           test -n "$EXPECTED_BOT_ID"
  50:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  51:           test "$GITHUB_ACTOR" = "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  52:           test "$SENDER_TYPE" = "Bot"
  53:           test "$SENDER_ID" = "$EXPECTED_BOT_ID"
  54:           git rev-parse --verify --quiet "$TRIGGER_HEAD^{commit}" >/dev/null

  56:           git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
  57:           git config user.email \
  58:             "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
  59: 
  60:           retry_delay() {
  61:             sleep "$((attempt * 2))"
  62:           }
  63: 
  64:           for attempt in $(seq 1 20); do
  65:             git fetch --no-tags origin \
  66:               "+refs/heads/$BASE_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$BASE_BRANCH" \

  74:             fi

  76:             git checkout --detach "$latest"

 102:                 retry_delay
 103:                 continue
 104:               fi
 105:             fi

 107:             git fetch --no-tags origin \
 108:               "+refs/heads/$BASE_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$BASE_BRANCH" \

 128:             cmp --silent \

 130:             cmp --silent data/runs.json "$generated/runs.json"
 131:             rm -rf -- "$generated"
 132:             trap - EXIT
 133: 
 134:             git fetch --no-tags origin \
 135:               "+refs/heads/$BASE_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$BASE_BRANCH" \
 136:               "+refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$STAGING_BRANCH"
 137:             base="$(git rev-parse "origin/$BASE_BRANCH")"
 138:             remote_head="$(git rev-parse "origin/$STAGING_BRANCH")"

 140:               retry_delay
 141:               continue
 142:             fi
 143:             if git diff --quiet "$base" "$candidate" --; then
 144:               exit 0
 145:             fi
 146: 
 147:             mapfile -t pull_numbers < <(
 148:               gh pr list \

 150:                 --state open \
 151:                 --base "$BASE_BRANCH" \
 152:                 --head "$STAGING_BRANCH" \
 153:                 --limit 2 \
 154:                 --json number \
 155:                 --jq '.[].number'
 156:             )
 157:             if [ "${#pull_numbers[@]}" -gt 1 ]; then
 158:               exit 1
 159:             fi
 160:             if [ "${#pull_numbers[@]}" -eq 0 ]; then
 161:               if ! gh pr create \

 164:                 --head "$STAGING_BRANCH" \

 168:                 continue

 171:                 gh pr list \

 173:                   --state open \
 174:                   --base "$BASE_BRANCH" \
 175:                   --head "$STAGING_BRANCH" \
 176:                   --limit 2 \
 177:                   --json number \
 178:                   --jq '.[].number'
 179:               )
 180:               if [ "${#pull_numbers[@]}" -ne 1 ]; then
 181:                 retry_delay
 182:                 continue

 184:             fi
 185:             pull_number="${pull_numbers[0]}"

 193:               --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" \
 194:               --add-label "cayleypy-ingest-approved"
 195: 
 196:             identity="$(
 197:               gh pr view "$pull_number" \
 198:                 --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" \
 199:                 --json state,isCrossRepository,headRefName,baseRefName,headRefOid \
 200:                 --jq '[.state,.isCrossRepository,.headRefName,.baseRefName,.headRefOid] | @tsv'
 201:             )"

 206:             test "$pr_head" = "$STAGING_BRANCH"

 213:             git fetch --no-tags origin \

 215:             test "$(git rev-parse "origin/$STAGING_BRANCH")" = "$candidate"
 216:             python tools/validate_result.py \
 217:               --base "$base" \

 219:               --allow-generated-indexes
 220: 
 221:             if gh pr merge "$pull_number" \
 222:               --repo "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" \
 223:               --squash \
 224:               --match-head-commit "$candidate"; then
 225:               merged="$(

 239:             if git diff --quiet "$candidate" "origin/$BASE_BRANCH" --; then
 240:               exit 0
 241:             fi
 242:             retry_delay
 243:           done
 244: 
 245:           exit 1

  95: def valid_state(values: Any, n: int) -> bool:
  96:     return (
  97:         isinstance(values, list)
  98:         and len(values) == n
  99:         and all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value < n for value in values)
 100:     )
 101: 
 102: 
 103: def valid_permutation(values: Any, n: int) -> bool:
 104:     return (
 105:         valid_state(values, n)
 106:         and len(set(values)) == n
 107:     )
 108: 
 109: 
 110: def replay(
 111:     initial: list[int],
 112:     path: list[str],
 113:     generators: dict[str, list[int]],
 114:     n: int,
 115: ) -> list[int]:
 116:     state = list(initial)
 117:     for move in path:
 118:         permutation = generators.get(move)
 119:         if permutation is None:
 120:             error("REPLAY_MOVE", move)
 121:         state = [state[source] for source in permutation]
 122:     return state
 123: 
 124: 
 125: def invert_path(
 126:     path: list[str],
 127:     generators: dict[str, list[int]],
 128:     n: int,
 129: ) -> list[str]:
 130:     by_permutation: dict[tuple[int, ...], list[str]] = {}
 131:     for name, permutation in generators.items():
 132:         by_permutation.setdefault(tuple(permutation), []).append(name)
 133:     output: list[str] = []
 134:     for move in reversed(path):
 135:         permutation = generators.get(move)
 136:         if permutation is None:
 137:             error("REFLECTION_MOVE", move)
 138:         inverse = [0] * n
 139:         for target, source in enumerate(permutation):
 140:             inverse[source] = target
 141:         names = by_permutation.get(tuple(inverse), [])
 142:         if len(names) != 1:
 143:             error("REFLECTION_INVERSE", move)
 144:         output.append(names[0])
 145:     return output
 146:

 148: def semantic(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
 149:     omitted = {
 150:         "client_submission_id",
 151:         "run_id",
 152:         "idempotency_key",
 153:         "submitted_at",
 154:     }
 155:     return {
 156:         key: value
 157:         for key, value in envelope.items()
 158:         if key not in omitted
 159:     }
 160:

 162: def validate_integrity(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
 163:     if len(canonical(envelope).encode("utf-8")) > MAX_ENVELOPE_BYTES:
 164:         error("ENVELOPE_SIZE")
 165:     proof = envelope["proof"]
 166:     manifest = envelope["model"]["manifest"]
 167:     n = manifest["state_len"]
 168:     if n < 1 or n > MAX_STATE_LENGTH or manifest["num_classes"] != n:
 169:         error("STATE_LENGTH")
 170:     generators = proof["generators"]
 171:     if len(generators) > MAX_MOVE_COUNT:
 172:         error("PROOF_BOUNDS")
 173:     if any(
 174:         not valid_permutation(permutation, n)
 175:         for permutation in generators.values()
 176:     ):
 177:         error("PERMUTATION")
 178:     if (
 179:         not valid_state(proof["initial_state"], n)
 180:         or not valid_state(proof["central_state"], n)
 181:     ):
 182:         error("STATE_PERMUTATION")
 183:     if len(envelope["solution"]["path"]) > MAX_PATH_LENGTH:
 184:         error("PROOF_BOUNDS")
 185:     hashes = (
 186:         ("initial_state", proof["initial_state_sha256"]),
 187:         ("central_state", proof["central_state_sha256"]),
 188:         ("generators", proof["generators_sha256"]),
 189:     )
 190:     for field, claimed in hashes:
 191:         if digest(proof[field]) != claimed:
 192:             error("PROOF_HASH", field)
 193:     solution = envelope["solution"]
 194:     if solution["length"] != len(solution["path"]):
 195:         error("SOLUTION_LENGTH")
 196:     reached = replay(
 197:         proof["initial_state"],
 198:         solution["path"],
 199:         generators,
 200:         n,
 201:     )
 202:     if (
 203:         digest(reached) != proof["reached_state_sha256"]
 204:         or reached != proof["central_state"]
 205:     ):
 206:         error("REPLAY_TARGET")
 207:     orientation = envelope["orientation"]
 208:     reflected_fields = {
 209:         "searched_path",
 210:         "reflected_source_path",
 211:         "reflected_source_sha256",
 212:     }
 213:     if orientation["final_orientation"] == "reflected":
 214:         if not reflected_fields <= set(orientation):
 215:             error("REFLECTION_PROVENANCE")
 216:         source = orientation["reflected_source_path"]
 217:         if (
 218:             digest_text(".".join(source))
 219:             != orientation["reflected_source_sha256"]
 220:         ):
 221:             error("REFLECTION_HASH")
 222:         if (
 223:             replay(proof["initial_state"], source, generators, n)
 224:             != proof["central_state"]
 225:         ):
 226:             error("REFLECTION_REPLAY")
 227:         searched = replay(
 228:             replay(proof["central_state"], source, generators, n),
 229:             orientation["searched_path"],
 230:             generators,
 231:             n,
 232:         )
 233:         if searched != proof["central_state"]:
 234:             error("REFLECTION_REPLAY")
 235:         if (
 236:             invert_path(orientation["searched_path"], generators, n)
 237:             != solution["path"]
 238:         ):
 239:             error("REFLECTION_INVERSE")
 240:     elif reflected_fields & set(orientation):
 241:         error("REFLECTION_PROVENANCE")

 243:     output_dim = manifest["output_dim"]

 249:         model_class == "output_move_count"

 252:         error("MODEL_HEAD")

 282: def validate_record(
 283:     root: Path,
 284:     head: str,
 285:     relative: str,
 286:     validator: Draft202012Validator,
 287: ) -> tuple[str, str]:
 288:     try:
 289:         raw = git(root, "show", f"{head}:{relative}", text=False)
 290:     except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
 291:         error("READ", str(exc))
 292:     if len(raw) > MAX_ENVELOPE_BYTES + 4096:
 293:         error("RECORD_SIZE")
 294:     try:
 295:         decoded = raw.decode("utf-8")
 296:         record = json.loads(decoded)
 297:     except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
 298:         error("JSON")
 299:     if decoded != canonical(record):
 300:         error("RECORD_CANONICAL")
 301:     if (
 302:         not isinstance(record, dict)
 303:         or set(record) != {"submission_id", "envelope"}
 304:         or not isinstance(record.get("submission_id"), str)
 305:         or not isinstance(record.get("envelope"), dict)
 306:     ):
 307:         error("WRAPPER")
 308:     submission_id = record["submission_id"]
 309:     envelope = record["envelope"]
 310:     validate_schema(envelope, validator)
 311:     validate_path(relative, submission_id, envelope)
 312:     validate_integrity(envelope)
 313:     return submission_id, envelope["idempotency_key"]

 316: def git(root: Path, *args: str, text: bool = True) -> str | bytes:
 317:     return subprocess.check_output(
 318:         ["git", "-C", str(root), *args],
 319:         text=text,
 320:     )
 321: 
 322: 
 323: def diff_changes(
 324:     root: Path, base: str, head: str
 325: ) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
 326:     raw = git(
 327:         root,
 328:         "diff",
 329:         "--name-status",
 330:         "-z",
 331:         "--no-renames",
 332:         f"{base}..{head}",
 333:         text=False,
 334:     )
 335:     fields = raw.split(b"\0")
 336:     if fields[-1] != b"" or (len(fields) - 1) % 2:
 337:         error("DIFF_PARSE")
 338:     changes: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
 339:     for index in range(0, len(fields) - 1, 2):
 340:         try:
 341:             status = fields[index].decode("ascii")
 342:             relative = fields[index + 1].decode(
 343:                 "utf-8", "surrogateescape"
 344:             )
 345:         except UnicodeDecodeError:
 346:             error("DIFF_PARSE")
 347:         changes.append((status, relative))
 348:     return changes
 349: 
 350: 
 351: def has_surrogate(value: str) -> bool:
 352:     return any(
 353:         0xDC80 <= ord(character) <= 0xDCFF
 354:         for character in value
 355:     )
 356: 
 357: 
 358: def head_result_paths(root: Path, head: str) -> Iterable[str]:
 359:     raw = git(
 360:         root, "ls-tree", "-rz", "--full-tree", head, text=False
 361:     )
 362:     for entry in raw.split(b"\0"):
 363:         if not entry:
 364:             continue
 365:         try:
 366:             metadata, path_bytes = entry.split(b"\t", 1)
 367:             mode, kind, _sha = metadata.split(b" ", 2)
 368:             relative = path_bytes.decode(
 369:                 "utf-8", "surrogateescape"
 370:             )
 371:         except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
 372:             error("HEAD_TREE")
 373:         if relative.startswith("results/v1/"):
 374:             if has_surrogate(relative) or not RESULT_PATH.fullmatch(
 375:                 relative
 376:             ):
 377:                 error("HEAD_PATH")
 378:             if mode != b"100644" or kind != b"blob":
 379:                 error("HEAD_MODE", relative)
 380:             yield relative
 381: 
 382: 
 383: def validate_range(
 384:     root: Path,
 385:     base: str,
 386:     head: str,
 387:     *,
 388:     allow_generated_indexes: bool = False,
 389: ) -> None:
 390:     validator = load_schema(root)
 391:     changes = diff_changes(root, base, head)
 392:     for status, relative in changes:
 393:         if allow_generated_indexes and relative in GENERATED_INDEX_PATHS:
 394:             if status not in {"A", "M"}:
 395:                 error("DIFF_APPEND_ONLY", f"{status}:{relative}")
 396:             mode_tokens = git(
 397:                 root, "ls-tree", head, "--", relative
 398:             ).split()
 399:             if not mode_tokens or mode_tokens[0] != "100644":
 400:                 error("DIFF_MODE", relative)
 401:             continue
 402:         if status != "A" or not RESULT_PATH.fullmatch(relative):
 403:             error("DIFF_APPEND_ONLY", f"{status}:{relative}")
 404:         mode_tokens = git(
 405:             root, "ls-tree", head, "--", relative
 406:         ).split()
 407:         if not mode_tokens or mode_tokens[0] != "100644":
 408:             error("DIFF_MODE", relative)
 409:     seen_ids: set[str] = set()
 410:     seen_idempotency: set[str] = set()
 411:     for relative in head_result_paths(root, head):
 412:         submission_id, idempotency = validate_record(
 413:             root, head, relative, validator
 414:         )
 415:         if submission_id in seen_ids:
 416:             error("DUPLICATE_SUBMISSION", submission_id)
 417:         if idempotency in seen_idempotency:
 418:             error("DUPLICATE_IDEMPOTENCY", idempotency)
 419:         seen_ids.add(submission_id)
 420:         seen_idempotency.add(idempotency)
 421: 
 422: 
 423: def main() -> int:
 424:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 425:     parser.add_argument("--base", required=True)
 426:     parser.add_argument("--head", required=True)
 427:     parser.add_argument("--allow-generated-indexes", action="store_true")
 428:     args = parser.parse_args()
 429:     root = Path.cwd().resolve()
 430:     try:
 431:         validate_range(
 432:             root,
 433:             args.base,
 434:             args.head,
 435:             allow_generated_indexes=args.allow_generated_indexes,
 436:         )
 437:     except (ValidationError, subprocess.CalledProcessError) as exc:
 438:         print(
 439:             "VALIDATION_ERROR "
 440:             f"{getattr(exc, 'code', 'GIT')} {exc}",
 441:             file=sys.stderr,
 442:         )
 443:         return 1
 444:     print("VALIDATION_OK")
 445:     return 0
 446: 
 447: 
 448: if __name__ == "__main__":
 449:     raise SystemExit(main())
### README.md
   1: # CayleyPy Beam Results
   2: 
   3: `ingest/staging` is an append-only staging branch for canonical CayleyPy v1 result records. The only accepted change type is an added regular file at:
   4: 
   5: `results/v1/<safe-competition>/<safe-puzzle-type>/<puzzle-id>/<UTC-date>/<uuidv7>.json`
   6: 
   7: The validator independently applies the exact v1 JSON Schema, canonical SHA-256/idempotency checks, bounded permutation replay, reflection provenance and inverse checks, model-head parity, and a complete `HEAD` duplicate scan. It rejects edits, deletions, symlinks, executable result files, non-server-derived paths, malformed wrappers, non-canonical record bytes, and records over the envelope limit.
   8: 
   9: Local gate:
  10: 
  11: ```bash
  12: python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
  13: python tools/validate_result.py --base <trusted-base-sha> --head <candidate-head-sha>
  14: ```
  15: 
  16: The repository stores only claimed author metadata; it does not authenticate authors. The staging workflow validates every pushed App commit independently and deterministically rebuilds only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`, `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json` from the latest full staging state. It retries index publication after concurrent commits. Auto-merge is disabled by default: it can run only after an operator sets `ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE=true`, sets the exact `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` repository variable, and applies the `cayleypy-ingest-approved` label to the same-repository staging PR.
  17: 
  18: Derived indexes are generated from the immutable records:
  19: 
  20: ```bash
  21: python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
  22: ```
  23: 
  24: The builder changes only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`,
  25: `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json`; existing legacy `data/`
  26: subdirectories and every `results/v1` record remain untouched. Index generation
  27: fails closed on unexpected result paths, malformed wrappers, duplicate
  28: submission/idempotency keys, and conflicting immutable run provenance. TSV

````

## 30. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T20:48:59.459Z` · source line `112`

```text
**Verdict: still BLOCK for a protected production repository.** The original self-trigger/auto-merge P0 is substantially addressed, but the final generated-index SHA has no Actions check because it is pushed with `GITHUB_TOKEN`; standard required-check protection will reject the merge. The design also still cannot guarantee 100 concurrent publications are accepted.

Prior findings now addressed:

- The reusable-workflow shape is valid: caller-job `permissions` and `uses` are compatible, and the callee cannot elevate beyond `contents/issues/pull-requests: write` ([validate-ingest.yml:60-71], [merge-ingest.yml:10-20]).
- App identity is materially stronger: login plus Bot sender type and immutable numeric sender ID are checked before validation/promotion ([validate-ingest.yml:38-47], [merge-ingest.yml:48-53]).
- The former `GITHUB_TOKEN` trigger loop is removed. Promotion happens synchronously inside the original trusted App-triggered run, with a lease, remote-head recheck, rebuild comparison, PR creation, label, and `--match-head-commit` merge ([merge-ingest.yml:64-145], [147-234]).
- No `gh pr checks --watch` remains, so the old explicit self-wait is gone.
- Generated-index mode is narrowly allowlisted to the four expected paths and only allows A/M regular files ([validate_result.py:28-33, 383-408]); the promotion path then byte-compares all generated outputs ([merge-ingest.yml:123-132]).

Remaining blockers:

1. **P0 — required checks remain impossible on the exact generated-index candidate SHA.**  
   The promotion job pushes its generated-index commit with `github.token` ([merge-ingest.yml:40, 98-104]). GitHub suppresses normal workflow events created by `GITHUB_TOKEN`, so `validate-ingest` does not run for `candidate`. The parent validation check belongs to `trigger_head`, not the new `candidate` SHA ([validate-ingest.yml:30-31, 69-71]). If `main` requires the validation check—which is the normal protected-branch configuration—`gh pr merge --match-head-commit "$candidate"` fails because the PR’s head has no required check. The workflow retries but cannot repair that state ([merge-ingest.yml:221-243]).  
   Required resolution: attach a trusted validation/check run to the final candidate SHA (or explicitly define and enforce a protected-branch bypass policy for this bot; that policy is currently neither implemented nor documented).

2. **P1 — 100 concurrent publisher acceptance is still not guaranteed.**  
   The 20-attempt lease loop serializes only index writers after a commit has reached staging ([merge-ingest.yml:64-114]). It does not serialize incoming App pushes or supply retry/queue semantics to the 100 publishers themselves. At most one simultaneous direct Git push wins; the rest need a producer-side non-fast-forward retry protocol that is outside this implementation. Even promotion workers may all exhaust their bounded collision retries during sustained publication.  
   So records already accepted are not overwritten (lease use is good), but “100 publications accepted without loss” is unproven and not provided.

3. **P1 — staging permanently diverges in commit graph after squash; unrelated `main` changes block future promotion.**  
   Squash merge copies staging’s tree into `main`, but does not make `main` an ancestor of `ingest/staging`. Later independent `main` changes appear as deletions/modifications in `git diff "$base" "$latest"` and are rejected by generated-index validation ([merge-ingest.yml:68-80], [validate_result.py:391-408]). There is no post-merge rebase/reset/reconciliation protocol. The next result can promote only while `main`’s tree differs from staging exclusively by allowed records/indexes.

4. **P1 — deployment-critical branch protection/bypass assumptions are absent.**  
   The job writes directly to `ingest/staging` and merges to `main` with `GITHUB_TOKEN` ([merge-ingest.yml:100-101, 221-224]). Protected branches commonly reject both unless Actions/bot bypass is configured. The source neither validates that repository policy nor documents the exact required setup; combined with blocker 1, normal required-check protection is incompatible.

Quality gaps:

- The tests are static-string contract tests; none simulates the critical candidate-SHA/no-event condition, a protected-branch merge, squash divergence plus an unrelated `main` commit, or 100 concurrent incoming pushes ([tests/test_promotion_contract.py:101-118], [tests/test_workflow_static.py:49-60]).
- README is stale: it says an operator applies the approval label ([README.md:16]), while the workflow now creates/applies it automatically ([merge-ingest.yml:187-194]); it omits the required bot ID and branch-protection/bypass contract.

Focused suite: `27 passed in 12.50s`. Passing tests do not cover the blockers above.


```

   1: name: promote-ingest

  20:     permissions:
  21:       checks: write
  22:       contents: write
  23:       issues: write
  24:       pull-requests: write
  25:     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
  26:     steps:
  27:       # actions/checkout v4.2.2
  28:       - uses: actions/checkout@11bd71901bbe5b1630ceea73d27597364c9af683
  29:         with:
  30:           fetch-depth: 0
  31:           ref: main
  32:       # actions/setup-python v5.4.0
  33:       - uses: actions/setup-python@e797f83bcb11b83ae66e0230d6156d7c80228e7c
  34:         with:
  35:           python-version: "3.12"
  36:           cache: pip
  37:           cache-dependency-path: requirements.txt
  38:       - run: python -m pip install --disable-pip-version-check -r requirements.txt
  39:       - env:
  40:           BASE_BRANCH: main
  41:           EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN: ${{ vars.RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN }}
  42:           EXPECTED_BOT_ID: ${{ vars.RESULTS_INGEST_BOT_ID }}
  43:           EXPECTED_REPOSITORY: TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results
  44:           GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
  45:           SENDER_ID: ${{ github.event.sender.id }}
  46:           SENDER_TYPE: ${{ github.event.sender.type }}
  47:           STAGING_BRANCH: ingest/staging
  48:           TRIGGER_HEAD: ${{ inputs.trigger_head }}
  49:         shell: bash
  50:         run: |
  51:           set -euo pipefail
  52:           test "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" = "$EXPECTED_REPOSITORY"
  53:           test -n "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  54:           test -n "$EXPECTED_BOT_ID"
  55:           case "$EXPECTED_BOT_ID" in
  56:             *[!0-9]*) exit 1 ;;
  57:           esac
  58: 
  59:           if [ "$GITHUB_EVENT_NAME" = "push" ]; then
  60:             test "$GITHUB_ACTOR" = "$EXPECTED_APP_LOGIN"
  61:             test "$SENDER_TYPE" = "Bot"
  62:             test "$SENDER_ID" = "$EXPECTED_BOT_ID"
  63:             test -n "$TRIGGER_HEAD"
  64:           elif [ "$GITHUB_EVENT_NAME" = "schedule" ] || \
  65:                [ "$GITHUB_EVENT_NAME" = "workflow_dispatch" ]; then
  66:             TRIGGER_HEAD=""
  67:           else
  68:             exit 1
  69:           fi
  70: 
  71:           git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
  72:           git config user.email \
  73:             "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
  74: 
  75:           retry_delay() {
  76:             sleep "$((attempt < 5 ? attempt : 5))"
  77:           }
  78: 
  79:           for attempt in $(seq 1 40); do
  80:             git fetch --no-tags origin \
  81:               "+refs/heads/$BASE_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$BASE_BRANCH" \
  82:               "+refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$STAGING_BRANCH"
  83:             base="$(git rev-parse "origin/$BASE_BRANCH")"
  84:             latest="$(git rev-parse "origin/$STAGING_BRANCH")"
  85:             if [ -n "$TRIGGER_HEAD" ]; then
  86:               git rev-parse --verify --quiet "$TRIGGER_HEAD^{commit}" >/dev/null
  87:               git merge-base --is-ancestor "$TRIGGER_HEAD" "$latest"
  88:             fi
  89: 
  90:             if git merge-base --is-ancestor "$latest" "$base"; then
  91:               if [ "$latest" != "$base" ]; then
  92:                 git push \
  93:                   --force-with-lease="refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH:$latest" \
  94:                   origin "$base:refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH"
  95:               fi
  96:               exit 0
  97:             fi
  98: 
  99:             common="$(git merge-base "$base" "$latest")"
 100:             python tools/validate_result.py \
 101:               --base "$common" \
 102:               --head "$latest" \
 103:               --allow-generated-indexes
 104: 
 105:             git checkout --detach "$latest"
 106:             if ! git merge-base --is-ancestor "$base" "$latest"; then
 107:               git merge --no-edit "$base"
 108:             fi
 109:             python tools/validate_result.py \
 110:               --base "$base" \
 111:               --head HEAD \
 112:               --allow-generated-indexes
 113:             python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
 114:             git add -- \
 115:               data/index.tsv \
 116:               data/by_author.tsv \
 117:               data/best_solutions.tsv \
 118:               data/runs.json
 119: 
 120:             while IFS= read -r changed; do
 121:               case "$changed" in
 122:                 data/index.tsv|data/by_author.tsv|\
 123:                 data/best_solutions.tsv|data/runs.json) ;;
 124:                 *) exit 1 ;;
 125:               esac
 126:             done < <(git diff --cached --name-only)
 127: 
 128:             if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
 129:               git commit -m "chore: rebuild CayleyPy result indexes"
 130:             fi
 131:             candidate="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
 132: 
 133:             if [ "$candidate" != "$latest" ]; then
 134:               if ! git push --force-with-lease="refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH:$latest" \
 135:                 origin "HEAD:refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH"; then
 136:                 retry_delay
 137:                 continue
 138:               fi
 139:             fi
 140: 
 141:             git fetch --no-tags origin \
 142:               "+refs/heads/$BASE_BRANCH:refs/remotes/origin/$BASE_BRANCH" \

 300:                 origin "$published:refs/heads/$STAGING_BRANCH" || true

 302:             fi

  27:     return {
  28:         "submission_id": envelope["client_submission_id"],
  29:         "envelope": envelope,

  33: def relative(item: dict) -> str:
  34:     envelope = item["envelope"]
  35:     return (
  36:         "results/v1/"
  37:         f"{validator.safe_segment(envelope['competition'])}/"
  38:         f"{validator.safe_segment(envelope['puzzle_type'])}/"
  39:         f"{envelope['puzzle_id']}/{envelope['submitted_at'][:10]}/"
  40:         f"{item['submission_id']}.json"
  41:     )

  43: 
  44: def commit(repo: Path, message: str) -> str:
  45:     subprocess.run(["git", "-C", repo, "add", "."], check=True)
  46:     subprocess.run(
  47:         ["git", "-C", repo, "commit", "-qm", message], check=True
  48:     )
  49:     return subprocess.check_output(
  50:         ["git", "-C", repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True
  51:     ).strip()
  52: 
  53: 
  54: def repository(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, str]:
  55:     repo = tmp_path / "repo"
  56:     repo.mkdir()
  57:     (repo / "schemas").mkdir()
  58:     shutil.copy(
  59:         ROOT / "schemas/result-v1.schema.json",
  60:         repo / "schemas/result-v1.schema.json",
  61:     )
  62:     subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", repo], check=True)
  63:     subprocess.run(
  64:         ["git", "-C", repo, "config", "user.email", "test@example.invalid"],
  65:         check=True,
  66:     )
  67:     subprocess.run(
  68:         ["git", "-C", repo, "config", "user.name", "Test"], check=True
  69:     )
  70:     (repo / "README.md").write_text("base\n", encoding="utf-8")
  71:     return repo, commit(repo, "base")
  72: 
  73:

  76:     item = record()
  77:     path = repo / relative(item)
  78:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
  79:     path.write_text(validator.canonical(item), encoding="utf-8")
  80:     (repo / "data").mkdir()

  82:     indexed_head = commit(repo, "raw and derived")
  83: 
  84:     with pytest.raises(validator.ValidationError, match="DIFF_APPEND_ONLY"):

  90:

  94:     bad_head = commit(repo, "unexpected derived file")

### README.md
   1: # CayleyPy Beam Results
   2: 
   3: `ingest/staging` is an append-only staging branch for canonical CayleyPy v1 result records. The only accepted change type is an added regular file at:
   4: 
   5: `results/v1/<safe-competition>/<safe-puzzle-type>/<puzzle-id>/<UTC-date>/<uuidv7>.json`
   6: 
   7: The validator independently applies the exact v1 JSON Schema, canonical SHA-256/idempotency checks, bounded permutation replay, reflection provenance and inverse checks, model-head parity, and a complete `HEAD` duplicate scan. It rejects edits, deletions, symlinks, executable result files, non-server-derived paths, malformed wrappers, non-canonical record bytes, and records over the envelope limit.
   8: 
   9: Local gate:
  10: 
  11: ```bash
  12: python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
  13: python tools/validate_result.py --base <trusted-base-sha> --head <candidate-head-sha>
  14: ```
  15: 
  16: The repository stores only claimed author metadata; it does not authenticate authors. The staging workflow validates every pushed App commit independently and deterministically rebuilds only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`, `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json` from the latest full staging state. It retries index publication after concurrent commits. Auto-merge is disabled by default: it can run only after an operator sets `ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE=true`, sets the exact `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` repository variable, and applies the `cayleypy-ingest-approved` label to the same-repository staging PR.
  17: 
  18: Derived indexes are generated from the immutable records:
  19: 
  20: ```bash
  21: python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
  22: ```
  23: 
  24: The builder changes only `data/index.tsv`, `data/by_author.tsv`,
  25: `data/best_solutions.tsv`, and `data/runs.json`; existing legacy `data/`
  26: subdirectories and every `results/v1` record remain untouched. Index generation
  27: fails closed on unexpected result paths, malformed wrappers, duplicate
  28: submission/idempotency keys, and conflicting immutable run provenance. TSV

  30:   if (!row || row.state !== "validated") return null;
  31:   const invalid = (): TerminalIntegrityError =>
  32:     new TerminalIntegrityError(id, "publication_raw_integrity_invalid");
  33:   const object = await env.RAW_RESULTS.get(row.raw_r2_key);
  34:   if (object === null || object.size > MAX_SERIALIZED_ENVELOPE_BYTES) throw invalid();
  35:   const raw = await object.text();
  36:   if (new TextEncoder().encode(raw).byteLength > MAX_SERIALIZED_ENVELOPE_BYTES) throw invalid();
  37:   const stored = object.customMetadata?.sha256;
  38:   if (
  39:     typeof stored !== "string" ||
  40:     !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(stored) ||
  41:     await sha256Hex(raw) !== stored
  42:   ) {
  43:     throw invalid();
  44:   }
  45:   let candidate: unknown;
  46:   try {
  47:     candidate = JSON.parse(raw);
  48:   } catch {
  49:     throw invalid();
  50:   }
  51:   const batch = validateBatch({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] });
  52:   if (!batch.ok) throw invalid();
  53:   const envelope = batch.value.results[0] as ResultEnvelopeV1;
  54:   if (
  55:     envelope.idempotency_key !== row.idempotency_key ||
  56:     envelope.run_id !== row.run_id ||
  57:     envelope.author.name !== row.author_name ||
  58:     envelope.competition !== row.competition ||
  59:     envelope.puzzle_type !== row.puzzle_type ||
  60:     envelope.puzzle_id !== row.puzzle_id ||
  61:     (await validateEnvelopeIntegrity(envelope)).length !== 0
  62:   ) {
  63:     throw invalid();
  64:   }
  65:   return {
  66:     id,
  67:     row,
  68:     envelope,
  69:     body: recordBody(id, envelope),
  70:     path: resultPath(id, envelope),
  71:   };
  72: }
  73: function sha(value: unknown): string | null { const candidate = value !== null && typeof value === "object" ? (value as Record<string, unknown>).sha : null; return typeof candidate === "string" && SHA_RE.test(candidate) ? candidate : null; }
  74: 
  75: function referenceSha(value: unknown): string | null {
  76:   if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return null;
  77:   const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  78:   return sha(record.object) ?? sha(record);
  79: }
  80: export class GitHubWriter extends DurableObject<GitHubWriterEnv> {
  81:   private alarmUpdate: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
  82:   constructor(ctx: DurableObjectState, env: GitHubWriterEnv) { super(ctx, env); ctx.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => { const entries = await ctx.storage.list<Pending>({ prefix: PENDING_PREFIX, limit: 1 }); if (entries.size && await ctx.storage.getAlarm() === null) await this.rearm(); }); }
  83:   private assertNormalMode(): void { if (resolveIngestMode(this.env.INGEST_MODE) !== "normal") throw new Error("writer_paused"); }
  84:   private async guardedRequest(path: string, options: Parameters<typeof githubRequest>[2]) { this.assertNormalMode(); return githubRequest(this.env, path, options); }
  85:   private async scheduleAlarmAt(deadline: number, force = false): Promise<void> { const update = this.alarmUpdate.then(async () => { const alarm = await this.ctx.storage.getAlarm(); if (force || alarm === null || alarm > deadline) await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(deadline); }); this.alarmUpdate = update.catch(() => undefined); await update; }
  86:   private async rearm(delayMs = RETAIN_DELAY_MS, force = false) { await this.scheduleAlarmAt(Date.now() + Math.min(MAX_DELAY_MS, Math.max(1, delayMs)), force); }
  87:   async enqueueValidated(submissionId: string): Promise<void> { if (!/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(submissionId)) throw new Error("writer_submission_invalid"); await this.ctx.storage.put(pendingKey(submissionId), { submissionId } satisfies Pending); await this.scheduleAlarmAt(Date.now() + RETAIN_DELAY_MS); }
  88:   async alarm(): Promise<void> { await this.flush(true); }
  89:   private async pending(): Promise<Pending[]> { const entries = await this.ctx.storage.list<Pending>({ prefix: PENDING_PREFIX, limit: MAX_RECORDS + 1 }); return [...entries.values()].slice(0, MAX_RECORDS); }
  90:   private async terminalize(error: TerminalIntegrityError): Promise<void> { const changed = await transition(this.env.RESULTS_DB, error.submissionId, ["validated"], "dead_letter", { safeError: error.safeError }); if (!changed) { const current = await findBySubmissionId(this.env.RESULTS_DB, error.submissionId); if (!current || current.state !== "dead_letter" || current.safe_error !== error.safeError) throw new Error("writer_state_conflict"); } await this.ctx.storage.delete(pendingKey(error.submissionId)); }
  91:   private async reconcile(value: Verified, commitSha: string): Promise<void> { const changed = await transition(this.env.RESULTS_DB, value.id, ["validated"], "staged", { safeError: null, githubPath: value.path, githubCommitSha: commitSha }); if (changed) return; const current = await findBySubmissionId(this.env.RESULTS_DB, value.id); if (!current || (current.state !== "staged" && current.state !== "published") || current.github_path !== value.path || !current.github_commit_sha || !SHA_RE.test(current.github_commit_sha)) throw new Error("writer_state_conflict"); }
  92:   async flush(forceFutureAlarm = false): Promise<FlushResult> { const pending = await this.pending(); if (!pending.length) return { staged: 0, retained: 0 }; if (resolveIngestMode(this.env.INGEST_MODE) !== "normal") { await this.rearm(RETAIN_DELAY_MS, forceFutureAlarm); return { staged: 0, retained: pending.length }; } const verified: Verified[] = []; let bytes = 0; try { for (const item of pending) { let value: Verified | null; try { value = await parseVerified(this.env, item.submissionId); } catch (error) { if (error instanceof TerminalIntegrityError) { await this.terminalize(error); continue; } throw error; } if (!value) { await this.ctx.storage.delete(pendingKey(item.submissionId)); continue; } const size = new TextEncoder().encode(value.body).byteLength; if (size > MAX_BYTES) { if (!await transition(this.env.RESULTS_DB, value.id, ["validated"], "rejected", { safeError: "publication_record_oversize" })) throw new Error("writer_state_conflict"); await this.ctx.storage.delete(pendingKey(value.id)); continue; } if (verified.length && bytes + size > MAX_BYTES) break; verified.push(value); bytes += size; } if (!verified.length) { const retained = (await this.pending()).length; if (retained) await this.rearm(RETAIN_DELAY_MS, forceFutureAlarm); return { staged: 0, retained }; } const repo = target(this.env); this.assertNormalMode(); const token = await getInstallationToken(this.env, Date.now(), fetch, () => this.assertNormalMode()); const committed = await this.commit(token, repo, verified); for (const error of committed.terminal) await this.terminalize(error); for (const value of committed.staged) { await this.reconcile(value, committed.sha); await this.ctx.storage.delete(pendingKey(value.id)); } const retained = (await this.pending()).length; if (retained) await this.rearm(RETAIN_DELAY_MS, forceFutureAlarm); return { staged: committed.staged.length, retained }; } catch { await this.rearm(RETAIN_DELAY_MS, forceFutureAlarm); throw new Error("github_writer_retryable"); } }
  93:   private async preflight(token: string, repo: Target, item: Verified): Promise<"add" | "same"> { const route = item.path.split("/").map(encodeURIComponent).join("/"); const found = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/contents/${route}?ref=${repo.branchQuery}`, { token }); if (found.status === 404) return "add"; const content = found.body !== null && typeof found.body === "object" ? (found.body as Record<string, unknown>).content : null; if (found.status !== 200 || typeof content !== "string") throw new Error("github_content_unavailable"); let decoded: string; try { decoded = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(content.replace(/\s/g, "")), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0))); } catch { throw new Error("github_content_unavailable"); } if (decoded !== item.body) throw new TerminalIntegrityError(item.id, "publication_path_conflict"); return "same"; }
  94:   private async commit(token: string, repo: Target, items: Verified[]): Promise<CommitResult> { const terminal: TerminalIntegrityError[] = []; for (let retry = 0; retry < 3; retry += 1) { const ref = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/git/ref/heads/${repo.branchRoute}`, { token }); const head = referenceSha(ref.body); if (ref.status !== 200 || !head) throw new Error("github_ref_unavailable"); const commit = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/git/commits/${head}`, { token }); const baseTree = commit.body !== null && typeof commit.body === "object" ? sha((commit.body as Record<string, unknown>).tree) : null; if (commit.status !== 200 || !baseTree) throw new Error("github_commit_unavailable"); const additions: Verified[] = [], staged: Verified[] = []; for (const item of items) { if (terminal.some((entry) => entry.submissionId === item.id)) continue; try { if (await this.preflight(token, repo, item) === "add") additions.push(item); staged.push(item); } catch (error) { if (error instanceof TerminalIntegrityError) terminal.push(error); else throw error; } } if (!staged.length || !additions.length) return { sha: head, staged, terminal }; const tree = additions.map((item) => ({ path: item.path, mode: "100644", type: "blob", content: item.body })); const newTree = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/git/trees`, { method: "POST", token, body: { base_tree: baseTree, tree } }); const treeSha = sha(newTree.body); if (newTree.status < 200 || newTree.status >= 300 || !treeSha) { if (newTree.status === 409 || newTree.status === 422) continue; throw new Error("github_tree_failed"); } const newCommit = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/git/commits`, { method: "POST", token, body: { message: `ingest: stage ${additions.length} CayleyPy results`, tree: treeSha, parents: [head] } }); const commitSha = sha(newCommit.body); if (newCommit.status < 200 || newCommit.status >= 300 || !commitSha) { if (newCommit.status === 409 || newCommit.status === 422) continue; throw new Error("github_commit_failed"); } const update = await this.guardedRequest(`/repos/${encodeURIComponent(repo.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(repo.repo)}/git/refs/heads/${repo.branchRoute}`, { method: "PATCH", token, body: { sha: commitSha, force: false } }); if (update.status >= 200 && update.status < 300) return { sha: commitSha, staged, terminal }; if (update.status !== 409 && update.status !== 422) throw new Error("github_ref_failed"); } throw new Error("github_ref_conflict"); }
  95: }

 231: async function flushWithRouter(
 232:   name: string,
 233:   seeded: Seeded,
 234:   existing: ExistingMode,
 235: ) {
 236:   const expectedBody = canonicalJson({
 237:     submission_id: seeded.submissionId,
 238:     envelope: seeded.envelope,
 239:   });
 240:   const router = githubRouter(existing, expectedBody);
 241:   vi.stubGlobal("fetch", router.fetcher);
 242:   const target = stub(name);
 243:   await target.enqueueValidated(seeded.submissionId);
 244:   const outcome = await runInDurableObject(
 245:     target,
 246:     async (instance, state) => {
 247:       const writer = instance as unknown as GitHubWriter;
 248:       configureWriter(writer);
 249:       resetInstallationTokenCacheForTest();
 250:       const result = await writer.flush();
 251:       return {
 252:         result,
 253:         pending: [
 254:           ...(await state.storage.list({ prefix: "pending/" })).keys(),
 255:         ],
 256:       };
 257:     },
 258:   );
 259:   return { ...router, ...outcome, expectedBody };
 260: }

## 46. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T20:58:47.612Z` · source line `157`

```text
**Verdict: clean on the prior P0/P1 protocol findings, with one deployment prerequisite.** The revised design now has a coherent protected-branch and eventual-reconciliation path. I found no new code-level blocker.

Resolved:

- **Required check on exact final SHA:** promotion creates a completed Check Run named `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` with `head_sha="$candidate"` only after full validation and byte-for-byte index regeneration ([merge-ingest.yml:152-180]). `checks: write` is correctly granted in both caller and reusable job ([validate-ingest.yml:72-77], [merge-ingest.yml:20-24]). This avoids reliance on workflow events from the `GITHUB_TOKEN` index push.
- **No required-check self-deadlock:** direct `--merge --match-head-commit "$candidate"` replaces auto-merge/check-watching ([merge-ingest.yml:277-301]). The candidate’s exact-head check is available before the PR merge request.
- **Squash divergence fixed:** promotion uses a regular merge, validates from the true merge base, merges `main` into staging when needed, rebuilds indexes, then requires `main` to be an ancestor of the candidate ([merge-ingest.yml:99-156]). After merge, staging is fast-forwarded to the published main merge commit with a lease ([merge-ingest.yml:293-300]); the next loop/schedule also performs this reconciliation ([lines 90-97]).
- **Race handling is materially sound:** every branch write uses `--force-with-lease`; remote head and ancestry are rechecked before Check Run, PR creation, and merge; any competing writer returns to the loop ([merge-ingest.yml:133-149, 182-190, 262-280]). The 10-minute schedule plus manual dispatch recover work after bounded attempts ([merge-ingest.yml:3-12, 79-80]).
- **Producer serialization/retry exists:** every validated record routes to one DO name, `cayleypy-results-v1` ([consumer.ts:47-49]); persistent pending keys deduplicate submissions and retain retryable work ([github-writer.ts:82-92]). Git ref updates use non-force PATCH and conflicts are retried/rearmed rather than dropped ([github-writer.ts:94]). The service test explicitly covers 100 concurrent unique enqueues retained in the DO ([github-writer.test.ts:297-315]).

Deployment prerequisite, not a code blocker: branch protection must require the exact Check Run name `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` for `main`, and permit the configured GitHub Actions token to write/reconcile `ingest/staging` (or grant it the applicable bypass). Do **not** require `validate-ingest / validate` on `main`, since that check is associated with the incoming App SHA rather than the generated candidate SHA. This should be documented in README/config before production use.

Quality notes:

- The generated-index validator remains narrowly scoped and is followed by deterministic output comparison; no regression there ([validate_result.py:383-420], [merge-ingest.yml:158-167]).
- The new protected-flow tests cover regular-merge ancestry and declarations of the exact Check Run/reconciler, though they remain mostly static rather than a GitHub API integration simulation ([test_protected_promotion.py:29-75]).
- Focused suite passed: **29 passed in 12.55s**.


```

- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.

- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.

- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
