const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Delete File: D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-beam-results-ingest-results\\README.md\n*** Add File: D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-beam-results-ingest-results\\README.md\n+# CayleyPy Beam Results\n+\n+This repository is the public, append-only record store for canonical\n+CayleyPy v1 results. `ingest/staging` is the trusted publication branch;\n+`main` contains only fully validated, exactly indexed publications.\n+\n+## Record contract\n+\n+Normal producer commits may add only one or more regular files at:\n+\n+```\n+results/v1/<safe-competition>/<safe-puzzle-type>/<puzzle-id>/<UTC-date>/<uuidv7>.json\n+```\n+\n+Existing result records are immutable: edits, deletions, renames, symlinks,\n+executable files, non-server-derived paths, malformed wrappers, non-canonical\n+JSON bytes, duplicate submission/idempotency keys, and oversized envelopes are\n+rejected. The validator also applies the v1 JSON Schema, canonical SHA-256 and\n+idempotency checks, bounded permutation replay, reflection provenance/inverse\n+checks, model-head parity, and a complete `HEAD` duplicate scan.\n+\n+The only non-record files the promotion system may change are deterministic\n+derivatives:\n+\n+```\n+data/index.tsv\n+data/by_author.tsv\n+data/best_solutions.tsv\n+data/runs.json\n+```\n+\n+They are rebuilt from the complete `results/` tree. A generated-index change\n+is accepted only in promotion mode, is limited to this allowlist, and is\n+byte-compared with a fresh rebuild before it can be published. Legacy `data/`\n+subdirectories and all `results/v1` records remain untouched.\n+\n+## Trusted publisher and authorship\n+\n+Only the configured GitHub App may publish normal staging commits. The push\n+workflow checks all of the following before it reads a record:\n+\n+- repository is `TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results` and ref is\n+  `ingest/staging`;\n+- `GITHUB_ACTOR` equals `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN`;\n+- the event sender is a GitHub `Bot` with numeric ID\n+  `RESULTS_INGEST_BOT_ID`.\n+\n+This authenticates the publishing App identity, not a human author. Author\n+metadata inside a result is a claim made by the producer and is deliberately\n+not treated as identity proof.\n+\n+The producer service serializes every validated submission through one\n+Durable Object queue. Pending submission IDs are durable and deduplicated;\n+GitHub ref conflicts retain work for retry rather than discarding a result.\n+The App creates ordinary non-force commits, while the promotion workflow uses\n+explicit `--force-with-lease` compare-and-swap writes only for deterministic\n+index/reconciliation commits. Thus concurrent publishers cannot overwrite an\n+accepted record.\n+\n+## Exact-candidate promotion\n+\n+For each trusted App push, `validate-ingest` validates the pushed append-only\n+range. When promotion is enabled, the reusable `promote-ingest` workflow then:\n+\n+1. fetches the latest staging and main heads and retries if the triggering\n+   commit is no longer an ancestor of staging;\n+2. reconciles staging with `main` using a regular merge when necessary,\n+   validates the merged range, and deterministically rebuilds the four indexes;\n+3. publishes that exact candidate only with a lease, then re-fetches and\n+   re-validates the same candidate;\n+4. byte-compares every checked-in index with a clean rebuild and creates the\n+   completed Check Run `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` on that candidate SHA;\n+5. creates or updates the same-repository `ingest/staging` to `main` PR,\n+   applies `cayleypy-ingest-approved`, and performs a regular merge guarded by\n+   `--match-head-commit`.\n+\n+Any concurrent push changes the head lease or PR head and causes a retry; it\n+cannot cause a stale candidate to merge. After a successful regular merge,\n+staging is fast-forwarded to the published main commit when its lease still\n+matches. The workflow also runs every ten minutes and supports\n+`workflow_dispatch`, so a transient GitHub conflict or failed post-merge\n+fast-forward is reconciled without losing a staged record.\n+\n+The exact-candidate Check Run is the protection check for the final generated\n+SHA. The ordinary `validate-ingest / validate` job belongs to the incoming App\n+push SHA and must not be made a required check for `main`.\n+\n+## Deployment prerequisites\n+\n+Before enabling promotion, configure the repository as follows:\n+\n+1. Set repository variables:\n+   `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` to the App bot login,\n+   `RESULTS_INGEST_BOT_ID` to its numeric GitHub bot ID, and\n+   `ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE` to `true`.\n+2. Allow the configured GitHub App to append normal commits to\n+   `ingest/staging`.\n+3. Allow GitHub Actions' repository token to write/reconcile\n+   `ingest/staging`, create/update PRs and labels, create Check Runs, and\n+   merge the approved PR. If branch protection normally blocks these actions,\n+   configure the narrow GitHub Actions/bot bypass required for these operations.\n+4. Protect `main` by requiring the exact check name\n+   `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` on the PR head. Do not require\n+   `validate-ingest / validate` for `main`, because it is not attached to the\n+   derived candidate SHA.\n+5. Keep the workflows on the default branch so scheduled and manual\n+   reconciliation are available.\n+\n+## Local verification\n+\n+Install the pinned dependency and validate a candidate range:\n+\n+```bash\n+python -m pip install -r requirements.txt\n+python tools/validate_result.py --base <trusted-base-sha> --head <candidate-head-sha>\n+```\n+\n+Promotion-mode validation permits only the four derived index paths in addition\n+to append-only records:\n+\n+```bash\n+python tools/validate_result.py --base <main-sha> --head <candidate-sha> --allow-generated-indexes\n+python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data\n+```\n*** End Patch";
const r = await tools.apply_patch(patch); text(typeof r === "string" ? r : JSON.stringify(r));

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index f63c681..4750240 100644
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -1 +1,70 @@
 # CayleyPy Beam Results
+
+This repository is the public, append-only record store for canonical CayleyPy v1 results. `ingest/staging` is the trusted publication branch; `main` contains only fully validated, exactly indexed publications.
+
+## Record contract
+
+Normal producer commits may add only regular files at:
+
+```
+results/v1/<safe-competition>/<safe-puzzle-type>/<puzzle-id>/<UTC-date>/<uuidv7>.json
+```
+
+Existing records are immutable: edits, deletions, renames, symlinks, executable files, malformed wrappers, non-canonical JSON, duplicate submission/idempotency keys, and oversized envelopes are rejected. The validator applies the v1 JSON Schema, canonical SHA-256/idempotency checks, bounded permutation replay, reflection provenance/inverse checks, model-head parity, and a complete `HEAD` duplicate scan.
+
+Only the promotion system may modify these deterministic derivatives:
+
+```
+data/index.tsv
+data/by_author.tsv
+data/best_solutions.tsv
+data/runs.json
+```
+
+They are rebuilt from the complete `results/` tree. Promotion mode permits only this allowlist and byte-compares every checked-in derivative with a fresh rebuild. Legacy `data/` subdirectories and every `results/v1` record remain untouched.
+
+## Trusted publisher and authorship
+
+Only the configured GitHub App may publish normal staging commits. Before reading a record, the push workflow verifies the repository/ref, `GITHUB_ACTOR == RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN`, and that the event sender is a GitHub `Bot` whose numeric ID equals `RESULTS_INGEST_BOT_ID`.
+
+This authenticates the publishing App, not a human author. Author metadata inside a record remains a producer claim and is not identity proof.
+
+The producer service routes every validated submission through one Durable Object queue. Pending IDs are durable and deduplicated; GitHub ref conflicts retain work for retry rather than dropping it. The App writes ordinary non-force commits. Promotion uses explicit `--force-with-lease` compare-and-swap writes only for deterministic index/reconciliation commits, so concurrent publishers cannot overwrite an accepted record.
+
+## Exact-candidate promotion
+
+For each trusted App push, `validate-ingest` validates the append-only range. When enabled, the reusable `promote-ingest` workflow then:
+
+1. Fetches the latest staging and main heads and retries when the triggering commit is no longer an ancestor of staging.
+2. Reconciles staging with `main` using a regular merge when necessary, validates the merged range, and rebuilds the four indexes deterministically.
+3. Publishes only with a lease, then re-fetches and re-validates the exact candidate SHA.
+4. Byte-compares all indexes with a clean rebuild and creates the completed Check Run `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` on that SHA.
+5. Creates or updates the same-repository `ingest/staging` to `main` PR, applies `cayleypy-ingest-approved`, and performs a regular merge guarded by `--match-head-commit`.
+
+A concurrent publication changes the head lease or PR head and causes a retry; it cannot merge a stale candidate. After a successful regular merge, staging is fast-forwarded to the published main commit when its lease still matches. Scheduled reconciliation runs every ten minutes, and `workflow_dispatch` is available for manual recovery.
+
+The exact-candidate Check Run is the protection check for the final generated SHA. `validate-ingest / validate` belongs to the incoming App-push SHA and must not be required for `main`.
+
+## Deployment prerequisites
+
+Before enabling promotion:
+
+1. Set repository variables: `RESULTS_INGEST_APP_LOGIN` (App bot login), `RESULTS_INGEST_BOT_ID` (numeric GitHub bot ID), and `ENABLE_CAYLEYPY_AUTO_MERGE=true`.
+2. Allow the configured GitHub App to append normal commits to `ingest/staging`.
+3. Allow the GitHub Actions repository token to write/reconcile `ingest/staging`, create/update PRs and labels, create Check Runs, and merge the approved PR. Configure only the narrow Actions/bot bypass required if branch protection otherwise blocks these operations.
+4. Protect `main` by requiring the exact check name `cayleypy-results/exact-candidate` on the PR head. Do not require `validate-ingest / validate` for `main`.
+5. Keep the workflows on the default branch so scheduled and manual reconciliation remain available.
+
+## Local verification
+
+```bash
+python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+python tools/validate_result.py --base <trusted-base-sha> --head <candidate-head-sha>
+```
+
+Promotion-mode validation permits only the four derived index paths in addition to append-only records:
+
+```bash
+python tools/validate_result.py --base <main-sha> --head <candidate-sha> --allow-generated-indexes
+python tools/build_indexes.py --results results --out data
+```
